Ultimately, our approach shifts LLMs from passive predictors to state-aware agents where reasoning becomes a stateful and manageable process.

</details>


### [113] [Stop Unnecessary Reflection: Training LRMs for Efficient Reasoning with Adaptive Reflection and Length Coordinated Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12113)
*Zewei Yu,Lirong Gao,Yuke Zhu,Bo Zheng,Sheng Guo,Haobo Wang,Junbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARLCP框架通过自适应反射惩罚和长度协调惩罚，动态平衡推理效率与准确性，显著减少大型推理模型的冗余反思步骤，在保持精度的同时大幅降低响应长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型在复杂推理任务中常产生过长的思维链，包含大量重复自我提问和循环推理等冗余反思，导致高token消耗、计算开销和延迟增加，但准确率并未提升，尤其在较小模型中问题更突出。

Method: 提出自适应反射与长度协调惩罚（ARLCP）强化学习框架，包含两个核心创新：1）自适应反射惩罚，动态削减不必要的反思步骤同时保留必要推理；2）根据问题估计复杂度校准的长度惩罚。通过协调这两种惩罚，鼓励模型生成更简洁有效的推理路径。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准上使用DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B和7B模型评估：1.5B模型平均响应长度减少53.1%，准确率提升5.8%；7B模型长度减少35.0%，准确率提升2.7%，实现了优于现有方法的效率-准确性权衡。

Conclusion: ARLCP框架能有效解决大型推理模型中冗余反思导致的效率问题，通过动态平衡推理效率与解决方案准确性，为资源受限环境中的高效推理提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance on complex reasoning tasks by employing test-time scaling. However, they often generate over-long chains-of-thought that, driven by substantial reflections such as repetitive self-questioning and circular reasoning, lead to high token consumption, substantial computational overhead, and increased latency without improving accuracy, particularly in smaller models. Our observation reveals that increasing problem complexity induces more excessive and unnecessary reflection, which in turn reduces accuracy and increases token overhead. To address this challenge, we propose Adaptive Reflection and Length Coordinated Penalty (ARLCP), a novel reinforcement learning framework designed to dynamically balance reasoning efficiency and solution accuracy. ARLCP introduces two key innovations: (1) a reflection penalty that adaptively curtails unnecessary reflective steps while preserving essential reasoning, and (2) a length penalty calibrated to the estimated complexity of the problem. By coordinating these penalties, ARLCP encourages the model to generate more concise and effective reasoning paths. We evaluate our method on five mathematical reasoning benchmarks using DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B models. Experimental results show that ARLCP achieves a superior efficiency-accuracy trade-off compared to existing approaches. For the 1.5B model, it reduces the average response length by 53.1% while simultaneously improving accuracy by 5.8%. For the 7B model, it achieves a 35.0% reduction in length with a 2.7% accuracy gain. The code is released at https://github.com/ZeweiYu1/ARLCP .

</details>


### [114] [HLA: Hadamard Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12128)
*Hanno Ackermann,Hong Cai,Mohsen Ghafoorian,Amirhossein Habibian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Hadamard线性注意力(HLA)，通过在高维空间计算相似度后应用非线性，比传统线性注意力能更好地近似softmax，在视频生成等大规模token任务中表现高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二次注意力计算成本高，现有线性注意力虽然高效但使用低阶有理函数近似softmax，近似质量有限。需要一种既能保持计算效率又能更好近似softmax的注意力机制。

Method: 提出Hadamard线性注意力(HLA)：1) 不像传统线性注意力在输入前分别对queries和keys应用非线性，而是在计算pairwise相似度后应用非线性；2) 该方法对应高阶有理函数近似softmax；3) 推导出与标准线性注意力类似的高效计算方案，无需耗时的张量重塑操作。

Result: HLA能更准确地近似softmax注意力，同时保持线性计算复杂度。在视频生成的扩散transformer模型（涉及大量tokens）中验证了方法的有效性，展示了其在大规模应用中的实用性。

Conclusion: HLA提供了一种新颖的线性注意力变体，通过在高维空间计算相似度后应用非线性，实现了对softmax的更好近似，同时保持了计算效率，特别适合大规模token应用场景。

Abstract: The attention mechanism is an important reason for the success of transformers. It relies on computing pairwise relations between tokens. To reduce the high computational cost of standard quadratic attention, linear attention has been proposed as an efficient approximation. It employs kernel functions that are applied independently to the inputs before the pairwise similarities are calculated. That allows for an efficient computational procedure which, however, amounts to a low-degree rational function approximating softmax.
  We propose Hadamard Linear Attention (HLA). Unlike previous works on linear attention, the nonlinearity in HLA is not applied separately to queries and keys, but, analogously to standard softmax attention, after the pairwise similarities have been computed. It will be shown that the proposed nonlinearity amounts to a higher-degree rational function to approximate softmax. An efficient computational scheme for the proposed method is derived that is similar to that of standard linear attention. In contrast to other approaches, no time-consuming tensor reshaping is necessary to apply the proposed algorithm. The effectiveness of the approach is demonstrated by applying it to a large diffusion transformer model for video generation, an application that involves very large amounts of tokens.

</details>


### [115] [Neutral Prompts, Non-Neutral People: Quantifying Gender and Skin-Tone Bias in Gemini Flash 2.5 Image and GPT Image 1.5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12133)
*Roberto Balestri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究量化了Gemini Flash 2.5 Image和GPT Image 1.5两个商业图像生成器中的性别和肤色偏见，发现中性提示词会产生高度极化的默认输出，而非中性的人口统计学结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试"中性提示词产生中性人口统计学输出"的假设，量化商业图像生成器中的性别和肤色偏见，为算法视觉文化提供审计框架。

Method: 使用四个语义中性提示词生成3,200张逼真图像，采用混合颜色归一化、面部标志点掩蔽和感知均匀肤色量化（使用Monk、PERLA和Fitzpatrick量表）的严格分析流程。

Result: 中性提示词产生高度极化的默认输出：两个模型都表现出强烈的"默认白人"偏见（>96%输出），但在性别上分歧明显：Gemini偏好女性主体，GPT偏好肤色较浅的男性主体。

Conclusion: 中性提示词作为诊断探针而非中性指令，挑战了"未标记语言导致包容性表征"的社会语言学假设，为审计算法视觉文化提供了稳健框架。

Abstract: This study quantifies gender and skin-tone bias in two widely deployed commercial image generators - Gemini Flash 2.5 Image (NanoBanana) and GPT Image 1.5 - to test the assumption that neutral prompts yield demographically neutral outputs. We generated 3,200 photorealistic images using four semantically neutral prompts. The analysis employed a rigorous pipeline combining hybrid color normalization, facial landmark masking, and perceptually uniform skin tone quantification using the Monk (MST), PERLA, and Fitzpatrick scales. Neutral prompts produced highly polarized defaults. Both models exhibited a strong "default white" bias (>96% of outputs). However, they diverged sharply on gender: Gemini favored female-presenting subjects, while GPT favored male-presenting subjects with lighter skin tones. This research provides a large-scale, comparative audit of state-of-the-art models using an illumination-aware colorimetric methodology, distinguishing aesthetic rendering from underlying pigmentation in synthetic imagery. The study demonstrates that neutral prompts function as diagnostic probes rather than neutral instructions. It offers a robust framework for auditing algorithmic visual culture and challenges the sociolinguistic assumption that unmarked language results in inclusive representation.

</details>


### [116] [Value Alignment Tax: Measuring Value Trade-offs in LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12134)
*Jiajun Chen,Hua Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出价值对齐税（VAT）框架，用于衡量对齐干预如何影响相互关联的价值系统，揭示传统目标导向评估中不可见的系统性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有价值对齐研究通常静态表征价值关系，忽略了提示、微调、偏好优化等干预措施如何重塑更广泛的价值系统。需要理解对齐压力下价值表达的动态变化。

Method: 引入价值对齐税（VAT）框架，测量对齐诱导的变化在相互关联的价值中的传播相对于目标增益的比例。使用基于Schwartz价值理论的受控场景-行动数据集，收集对齐前后的配对规范性判断，分析不同模型、价值和策略下的对齐效应。

Result: 对齐通常产生不均匀、结构化的价值共变运动。这些效应在传统仅针对目标的评估中不可见，揭示了系统性和过程层面的对齐风险。

Conclusion: VAT框架提供了理解LLM中价值对齐动态的新视角，揭示了传统评估方法无法捕捉的系统性风险，为更全面的价值对齐评估提供了工具。

Abstract: Existing work on value alignment typically characterizes value relations statically, ignoring how interventions - such as prompting, fine-tuning, or preference optimization - reshape the broader value system. We introduce the Value Alignment Tax (VAT), a framework that measures how alignment-induced changes propagate across interconnected values relative to achieved on-target gain. VAT captures the dynamics of value expression under alignment pressure. Using a controlled scenario-action dataset grounded in Schwartz value theory, we collect paired pre-post normative judgments and analyze alignment effects across models, values, and alignment strategies. Our results show that alignment often produces uneven, structured co-movement among values. These effects are invisible under conventional target-only evaluation, revealing systemic, process-level alignment risks and offering new insights into the dynamics of value alignment in LLMs.

</details>


### [117] [STAR : Bridging Statistical and Agentic Reasoning for Large Model Performance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12143)
*Xiaoxiao Wang,Chunxiao Li,Junying Wang,Yijin Guo,Zijian Chen,Chunyi Li,Xiaohong Liu,Zicheng Zhang,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STAR框架结合统计期望与知识驱动推理，通过约束概率矩阵分解和期望违背理论，在稀疏观测下预测大模型性能，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全面的大模型评估成本过高，而现有统计方法难以处理模式偏移、数据稀疏和缺乏解释性，纯LLM方法也不可靠，需要新的预测框架。

Method: STAR框架包含：1) 使用专门检索器获取外部知识；2) 将语义特征嵌入约束概率矩阵分解生成带不确定性的统计期望；3) 基于期望违背理论的推理模块，通过族内分析、跨模型比较和可信度感知聚合来优化预测。

Result: 在分数和排名指标上均优于所有基线方法，在极端稀疏条件下（每个测试模型仅1-2个观测分数）比最强统计方法总分数提升14.46%。

Conclusion: STAR成功融合统计期望与知识驱动推理，在稀疏观测下实现了准确、可解释的大模型性能预测，为解决昂贵评估问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: As comprehensive large model evaluation becomes prohibitively expensive, predicting model performance from limited observations has become essential. However, existing statistical methods struggle with pattern shifts, data sparsity, and lack of explanation, while pure LLM methods remain unreliable. We propose STAR, a framework that bridges data-driven STatistical expectations with knowledge-driven Agentic Reasoning. STAR leverages specialized retrievers to gather external knowledge and embeds semantic features into Constrained Probabilistic Matrix Factorization (CPMF) to generate statistical expectations with uncertainty. A reasoning module guided by Expectation Violation Theory (EVT) then refines predictions through intra-family analysis, cross-model comparison, and credibility-aware aggregation, producing adjustments with traceable explanations. Extensive experiments show that STAR consistently outperforms all baselines on both score-based and rank-based metrics, delivering a 14.46% gain in total score over the strongest statistical method under extreme sparsity, with only 1--2 observed scores per test model.

</details>


### [118] [Seq2Seq2Seq: Lossless Data Compression via Discrete Latent Transformers and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12146)
*Mahdi Khodabandeh,Ghazal Shabani,Arash Yousefi Jordehi,Seyed Abolghasem Mirroshandel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于强化学习和T5语言模型的新型无损压缩方法，将数据压缩为token序列而非传统向量表示，在保持语义完整性的同时获得更高压缩比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统压缩方法（如字典法和统计法）难以充分利用复杂数据结构中的冗余信息；现有深度学习方法依赖密集向量表示，掩盖了底层token结构。需要一种既能保持token结构又能实现高效压缩的新方法。

Method: 采用强化学习训练T5语言模型架构，将数据压缩为token序列而非连续潜在空间表示。使用离策略强化学习算法优化序列长度，最小化冗余并提升压缩效率。该方法独立于外部语法或世界知识。

Result: 相比传统方法，该方法在压缩比方面取得显著提升。通过利用语言模型中的潜在信息，系统能够有效压缩数据而无需显式内容理解。

Conclusion: 该方法为各种应用提供了更稳健实用的压缩解决方案，通过保持token结构实现更高压缩比和语义完整性，展示了强化学习在数据压缩领域的潜力。

Abstract: Efficient lossless compression is essential for minimizing storage costs and transmission overhead while preserving data integrity. Traditional compression techniques, such as dictionary-based and statistical methods, often struggle to optimally exploit the structure and redundancy in complex data formats. Recent advancements in deep learning have opened new avenues for compression; however, many existing approaches depend on dense vector representations that obscure the underlying token structure. To address these limitations, we propose a novel lossless compression method that leverages Reinforcement Learning applied to a T5 language model architecture. This approach enables the compression of data into sequences of tokens rather than traditional vector representations. Unlike auto-encoders, which typically encode information into continuous latent spaces, our method preserves the token-based structure, aligning more closely with the original data format. This preservation allows for higher compression ratios while maintaining semantic integrity. By training the model using an off-policy Reinforcement Learning algorithm, we optimize sequence length to minimize redundancy and enhance compression efficiency. Our method introduces an efficient and adaptive data compression system built upon advanced Reinforcement Learning techniques, functioning independently of external grammatical or world knowledge. This approach shows significant improvements in compression ratios compared to conventional methods. By leveraging the latent information within language models, our system effectively compresses data without requiring explicit content understanding, paving the way for more robust and practical compression solutions across various applications.

</details>


### [119] [GPT-4o Lacks Core Features of Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12150)
*John Muchovej,Amanda Royka,Shane Lee,Julian Jara-Ettinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs在简单心智理论任务中能近似人类判断，但在逻辑等价任务中失败，且行为预测与心理状态推断一致性低，表明其社交能力并非源于领域通用或一致的心智理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通过基准测试评估LLMs是否具备心智理论，但这些测试并未检验心智理论所假设的实际表征——即心理状态与行为的因果模型。需要基于认知科学定义开发新的评估框架来真正测试LLMs是否拥有连贯、领域通用且一致的心理状态-行为因果模型。

Method: 采用基于认知科学的心智理论定义，开发新的评估框架，测试LLMs是否拥有连贯、领域通用且一致的心理状态-行为因果模型。具体方法包括：1）在简单心智理论范式中测试LLMs近似人类判断的能力；2）在逻辑等价任务中测试LLMs的表现；3）评估LLMs行为预测与相应心理状态推断之间的一致性。

Result: 研究发现：1）LLMs在简单心智理论范式中能够成功近似人类判断；2）但在逻辑等价任务中失败；3）LLMs的行为预测与相应心理状态推断之间表现出较低的一致性。这些结果表明LLMs的社交熟练度并非源于领域通用或一致的心智理论。

Conclusion: 尽管LLMs在某些社交任务中表现出熟练度，但它们缺乏连贯、领域通用且一致的心理状态-行为因果模型。因此，LLMs展现的社交能力并非真正的心智理论，而是通过其他机制实现的近似表现。

Abstract: Do Large Language Models (LLMs) possess a Theory of Mind (ToM)? Research into this question has focused on evaluating LLMs against benchmarks and found success across a range of social tasks. However, these evaluations do not test for the actual representations posited by ToM: namely, a causal model of mental states and behavior. Here, we use a cognitively-grounded definition of ToM to develop and test a new evaluation framework. Specifically, our approach probes whether LLMs have a coherent, domain-general, and consistent model of how mental states cause behavior -- regardless of whether that model matches a human-like ToM. We find that even though LLMs succeed in approximating human judgments in a simple ToM paradigm, they fail at a logically equivalent task and exhibit low consistency between their action predictions and corresponding mental state inferences. As such, these findings suggest that the social proficiency exhibited by LLMs is not the result of an domain-general or consistent ToM.

</details>


### [120] [Sci-CoE: Co-evolving Scientific Reasoning LLMs via Geometric Consensus with Sparse Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12164)
*Xiaohan He,Shiyang Feng,Songtao Huang,Lei Bai,Bin Wang,Bo Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Sci-CoE是一个两阶段科学协同进化框架，通过从稀疏监督到无监督学习的过渡，使模型能够作为求解器和验证器自我进化，提升科学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在科学推理任务中仍然脆弱，主要原因是不可靠的解决方案评估和验证策略多样性有限。现有协同进化范式在代码和数学领域表现良好，但在科学推理中需要更可靠的评估和更多样化的验证方法。

Method: 提出两阶段科学协同进化框架：第一阶段使用少量标注数据为验证器建立基本正确性判断锚点；第二阶段引入几何奖励机制，综合考虑共识、可靠性和多样性，驱动大规模无标签数据上的自我迭代。

Result: 在多个通用科学基准测试上的实验表明，Sci-CoE增强了复杂推理能力，表现出强大的可扩展性，有助于构建更鲁棒和多样化的评估系统。

Conclusion: Sci-CoE通过协同进化框架有效解决了科学推理中评估不可靠和验证策略单一的问题，为构建更强大的科学推理系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated exceptional reasoning capabilities, and co-evolving paradigms have shown promising results in domains such as code and math. However, in scientific reasoning tasks, these models remain fragile due to unreliable solution evaluation and limited diversity in verification strategies. In this work, we propose Sci-CoE, a two-stage scientific co-evolving framework that enables models to self-evolve as both solver and verifier through a transition from sparse supervision to unsupervised learning. In the first stage, the model uses a small set of annotated data to establish fundamental correctness judgment anchors for the Verifier. In the second stage, we introduce a geometric reward mechanism that jointly considers consensus, reliability, and diversity, driving large-scale self-iteration on unlabeled data. Experiments on several general scientific benchmarks demonstrate that Sci-CoE enhances complex reasoning capabilities and exhibits strong scalability, facilitating the construction of more robust and diverse evaluation systems. Codes are available at https://github.com/InternScience/Sci-CoE.

</details>


### [121] [Pedagogically-Inspired Data Synthesis for Language Model Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12172)
*Bowei He,Yankai Chen,Xiaokun Zhang,Linghe Kong,Philip S. Yu,Xue Liu,Chen Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出IOA框架，基于教育原理（布鲁姆掌握学习、维果茨基最近发展区）进行LLM知识蒸馏，通过三阶段流程（知识识别、组织、适配）实现系统性教学式蒸馏，显著提升小模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM知识蒸馏方法缺乏教学意识，将知识转移视为一次性数据合成和训练任务，而不是系统性的学习过程。需要借鉴教育原理来改进蒸馏效果。

Method: 提出IOA三阶段框架：1) 知识识别器识别学生模型的知识缺陷；2) 知识组织器通过渐进式课程组织知识传递；3) 知识适配器调整表示以匹配学生模型的认知能力。整合布鲁姆掌握学习原则和维果茨基最近发展区理论，创建动态蒸馏过程。

Result: 使用LLaMA-3.1/3.2和Qwen2.5作为学生模型，IOA在DollyEval上保留教师模型94.7%的性能，参数量不到1/10。在复杂推理任务上表现突出：MATH提升19.2%，HumanEval提升22.3%，显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于教育原理的教学式蒸馏框架IOA有效提升了LLM知识蒸馏效果，特别是在复杂推理任务上表现优异，为高效AI系统部署提供了新方法。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation from Large Language Models (LLMs) to smaller models has emerged as a critical technique for deploying efficient AI systems. However, current methods for distillation via synthetic data lack pedagogical awareness, treating knowledge transfer as a one-off data synthesis and training task rather than a systematic learning process. In this paper, we propose a novel pedagogically-inspired framework for LLM knowledge distillation that draws from fundamental educational principles. Our approach introduces a three-stage pipeline -- Knowledge Identifier, Organizer, and Adapter (IOA) -- that systematically identifies knowledge deficiencies in student models, organizes knowledge delivery through progressive curricula, and adapts representations to match the cognitive capacity of student models. We integrate Bloom's Mastery Learning Principles and Vygotsky's Zone of Proximal Development to create a dynamic distillation process where student models approach teacher model's performance on prerequisite knowledge before advancing, and new knowledge is introduced with controlled, gradual difficulty increments. Extensive experiments using LLaMA-3.1/3.2 and Qwen2.5 as student models demonstrate that IOA achieves significant improvements over baseline distillation methods, with student models retaining 94.7% of teacher performance on DollyEval while using less than 1/10th of the parameters. Our framework particularly excels in complex reasoning tasks, showing 19.2% improvement on MATH and 22.3% on HumanEval compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [122] [SAM3-LiteText: An Anatomical Study of the SAM3 Text Encoder for Efficient Vision-Language Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12173)
*Chengxi Zeng,Yuxuan Jiang,Ge Gao,Shuai Wang,Duolikun Danier,Bin Zhu,Stevan Rudinac,David Bull,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAM3-LiteText：通过轻量级文本编码框架优化视觉语言分割模型，用紧凑的MobileCLIP学生模型替换原始SAM3文本编码器，参数减少88%的同时保持分割性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言分割模型（如SAM3）使用为开放语言理解设计的大型通用文本编码器，而实际分割提示通常简短、结构化且语义受限，导致文本编码器容量严重过度配置，产生持续的计算和内存开销

Method: 1) 对404,796个真实提示进行大规模解剖分析，发现上下文窗口利用率低、词汇使用稀疏、文本嵌入位于低维流形等冗余问题；2) 提出SAM3-LiteText框架，用紧凑的MobileCLIP学生模型替换原始SAM3文本编码器；3) 通过知识蒸馏优化轻量级文本编码器

Result: SAM3-LiteText将文本编码器参数减少高达88%，显著降低静态内存占用，同时在图像和视频分割基准测试中保持与原始模型相当的分割性能

Conclusion: 通过分析分割提示的特性并设计针对性的轻量级文本编码框架，可以在大幅减少参数和内存开销的同时保持视觉语言分割模型的性能，为高效视觉语言模型设计提供了新思路

Abstract: Vision-language segmentation models such as SAM3 enable flexible, prompt-driven visual grounding, but inherit large, general-purpose text encoders originally designed for open-ended language understanding. In practice, segmentation prompts are short, structured, and semantically constrained, leading to substantial over-provisioning in text encoder capacity and persistent computational and memory overhead. In this paper, we perform a large-scale anatomical analysis of text prompting in vision-language segmentation, covering 404,796 real prompts across multiple benchmarks. Our analysis reveals severe redundancy: most context windows are underutilized, vocabulary usage is highly sparse, and text embeddings lie on low-dimensional manifold despite high-dimensional representations. Motivated by these findings, we propose SAM3-LiteText, a lightweight text encoding framework that replaces the original SAM3 text encoder with a compact MobileCLIP student that is optimized by knowledge distillation. Extensive experiments on image and video segmentation benchmarks show that SAM3-LiteText reduces text encoder parameters by up to 88%, substantially reducing static memory footprint, while maintaining segmentation performance comparable to the original model. Code: https://github.com/SimonZeng7108/efficientsam3/tree/sam3_litetext.

</details>


### [123] ["Sorry, I Didn't Catch That": How Speech Models Miss What Matters Most](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12249)
*Kaitlyn Zhou,Martijn Bartelds,Federico Bianchi,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究发现，尽管语音识别系统在标准基准测试中表现良好，但在真实世界高风险的短语音转录任务（如美国街道名称转录）中失败率高达44%，且对非英语母语者的影响加倍。作者提出了一种使用开源TTS模型生成合成数据的方法，仅需不到1000个样本就能将非英语母语者的街道名称转录准确率提升近60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音识别系统虽然在标准基准测试中取得了低词错误率，但在真实世界部署中，对于高风险、短语音的转录任务（如街道名称转录）存在严重失败。这种失败对非英语母语者造成了不成比例的影响，可能导致导航错误等严重后果。

Method: 1) 评估了来自OpenAI、Deepgram、Google和Microsoft的15个模型在语言多样化的美国说话者录音上的表现；2) 量化了转录错误对地理位置的后续影响；3) 提出了一种合成数据生成方法，使用开源文本转语音模型生成命名实体的多样化发音；4) 使用不到1000个合成样本对模型进行微调。

Result: 1) 平均转录错误率为44%；2) 转录错误对所有说话者都造成系统性错误，但对非英语母语者的路由距离错误是英语母语者的两倍；3) 使用合成数据微调后，非英语母语者的街道名称转录准确率相对于基础模型提升了近60%。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了语音系统在基准测试表现和真实世界可靠性之间的关键差距，并展示了一种简单、可扩展的路径来减少高风险转录错误，特别是对非英语母语者的影响。合成数据生成方法能有效提升模型在多样化发音场景下的性能。

Abstract: Despite speech recognition systems achieving low word error rates on standard benchmarks, they often fail on short, high-stakes utterances in real-world deployments. Here, we study this failure mode in a high-stakes task: the transcription of U.S. street names as spoken by U.S. participants. We evaluate 15 models from OpenAI, Deepgram, Google, and Microsoft on recordings from linguistically diverse U.S. speakers and find an average transcription error rate of 44%. We quantify the downstream impact of failed transcriptions by geographic locations and show that mis-transcriptions systematically cause errors for all speakers, but that routing distance errors are twice as large for non-English primary speakers compared to English primary speakers. To mitigate this harm, we introduce a synthetic data generation approach that produces diverse pronunciations of named entities using open-source text-to-speech models. Fine-tuning with less than 1,000 synthetic samples improves street name transcription accuracy by nearly 60% (relative to base models) for non-English primary speakers. Our results highlight a critical gap between benchmark performance and real-world reliability in speech systems and demonstrate a simple, scalable path to reducing high-stakes transcription errors.

</details>


### [124] [Think like a Scientist: Physics-guided LLM Agent for Equation Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12259)
*Jianke Yang,Ohm Venkatachalam,Mohammad Kianezhad,Sharvaree Vadgama,Rose Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KeplerAgent是一个基于LLM的智能体框架，通过模拟科学家的多步推理过程（先推断物理性质如对称性，再作为先验约束方程空间）来发现符号方程，相比直接猜测的方法在符号准确性和噪声鲁棒性上表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的符号方程发现系统通常直接从数据猜测方程，而没有模拟科学家常用的多步推理过程：先推断物理性质（如对称性），再将这些性质作为先验来限制候选方程空间。这种直接猜测的方法可能无法充分利用物理洞察力。

Method: KeplerAgent是一个智能体框架，明确遵循科学推理过程：1）协调基于物理的工具提取中间结构（如对称性）；2）利用这些结果配置符号回归引擎（如PySINDy和PySR），包括函数库和结构约束；3）通过多步推理过程发现符号方程。

Result: 在一系列物理方程基准测试中，KeplerAgent相比LLM和传统基线方法，实现了显著更高的符号准确性和对噪声数据更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过明确建模科学家的多步推理过程（先推断物理性质再约束方程空间），KeplerAgent框架在符号方程发现任务上超越了直接猜测的方法，展示了将物理洞察与符号回归相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Explaining observed phenomena through symbolic, interpretable formulas is a fundamental goal of science. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have emerged as promising tools for symbolic equation discovery, owing to their broad domain knowledge and strong reasoning capabilities. However, most existing LLM-based systems try to guess equations directly from data, without modeling the multi-step reasoning process that scientists often follow: first inferring physical properties such as symmetries, then using these as priors to restrict the space of candidate equations. We introduce KeplerAgent, an agentic framework that explicitly follows this scientific reasoning process. The agent coordinates physics-based tools to extract intermediate structure and uses these results to configure symbolic regression engines such as PySINDy and PySR, including their function libraries and structural constraints. Across a suite of physical equation benchmarks, KeplerAgent achieves substantially higher symbolic accuracy and greater robustness to noisy data than both LLM and traditional baselines.

</details>


### [125] [CM2: Reinforcement Learning with Checklist Rewards for Multi-Turn and Multi-Step Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12268)
*Zhen Zhang,Kaiqiang Song,Xun Wang,Yebowen Hu,Weixiang Yan,Chenyang Zhao,Henry Peng Zou,Haoyun Deng,Sathish Reddy Indurthi,Shujian Liu,Simin Ma,Xiaoyang Wang,Xin Eric Wang,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CM2是一个RL框架，使用清单奖励替代可验证结果奖励，通过细粒度二元标准和结构化元数据优化多轮多步工具使用智能体，在LLM模拟环境中训练，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RL在现实AI智能体应用中面临挑战：真实目标缺乏可验证奖励，强调开放式行为；多轮多步工具使用的RL研究不足；构建和维护可执行工具环境成本高，限制了规模和覆盖范围。

Method: CM2使用清单奖励替代可验证结果奖励，将每轮预期行为分解为细粒度二元标准，包含显式证据基础和结构化元数据。采用稀疏奖励分配但密集评估标准的策略，在可扩展的LLM模拟工具环境中进行训练，避免大规模工具集的重工程。

Result: 从8B基础模型开始，在8k示例RL数据集上训练，CM2相比SFT对应模型在tau^-Bench上提升8分，在BFCL-V4上提升10分，在ToolSandbox上提升12分。结果匹配甚至优于类似规模的开源基线，包括评判模型。

Conclusion: CM2提供了一个可扩展的配方，用于优化多轮多步工具使用智能体，而不依赖可验证奖励，为现实AI智能体RL训练提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used to solve real-world tasks by reasoning over multi-turn user interactions and invoking external tools. However, applying reinforcement learning to such settings remains difficult: realistic objectives often lack verifiable rewards and instead emphasize open-ended behaviors; moreover, RL for multi-turn, multi-step agentic tool use is still underexplored; and building and maintaining executable tool environments is costly, limiting scale and coverage. We propose CM2, an RL framework that replaces verifiable outcome rewards with checklist rewards. CM2 decomposes each turn's intended behavior into fine-grained binary criteria with explicit evidence grounding and structured metadata, turning open-ended judging into more stable classification-style decisions. To balance stability and informativeness, our method adopts a strategy of sparse reward assignment but dense evaluation criteria. Training is performed in a scalable LLM-simulated tool environment, avoiding heavy engineering for large tool sets. Experiments show that CM2 consistently improves over supervised fine-tuning. Starting from an 8B Base model and training on an 8k-example RL dataset, CM2 improves over the SFT counterpart by 8 points on tau^-Bench, by 10 points on BFCL-V4, and by 12 points on ToolSandbox. The results match or even outperform similarly sized open-source baselines, including the judging model. CM2 thus provides a scalable recipe for optimizing multi-turn, multi-step tool-using agents without relying on verifiable rewards. Code provided by the open-source community: https://github.com/namezhenzhang/CM2-RLCR-Tool-Agent.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Data-Driven Hull-Fouling Cleaning Schedule Optimization to Reduce Carbon Footprint of Vessels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11248)
*Samuel Ward,Marah-Lisanne Thormann,Julian Wharton,Alain Zemkoho*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于动态规划的数据驱动方法优化船舶清洁计划，平衡环保与经济效益，基于真实传感器数据验证可减少5%燃油消耗


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国际海事组织要求减少航运温室气体排放，船公司需在保持经济可行性的同时降低船队CO2排放。定期清洁船体和螺旋桨是重要措施，但成本高昂，需要科学评估何时清洁在经济和环境上合理

Method: 提出新颖的数据驱动动态规划方法，优化船舶清洁计划，平衡环境和经济考量。基于真实世界的传感器数据进行数值实验验证

Result: 基于10艘不定期贸易船舶的传感器数据，在四年周期内，即使考虑增加1-2次清洁的成本，仍可减少高达5%的燃油消耗

Conclusion: 提出的数据驱动动态规划方法能有效优化船舶清洁计划，在满足环保法规的同时保持经济可行性，为航运业实现减排目标提供实用工具

Abstract: In response to climate change, the International Maritime Organization has introduced regulatory frameworks to reduce greenhouse gas emissions from international shipping. Compliance with these regulations is increasingly expected from individual shipping companies, compelling vessel operators to lower the CO2 emissions of their fleets while maintaining economic viability. An important step towards achieving this is performing regular hull and propeller cleaning; however, this entails significant costs. As a result, assessing whether ship performance has declined sufficiently to warrant cleaning from an environmental and economic standpoint is a critical task to ensure both long-term viability and regulatory compliance. In this paper, we address this challenge by proposing a novel data-driven dynamic programming approach to optimize vessel cleaning schedules by balancing both environmental and economic considerations. In numerical experiments, we demonstrate the usefulness of our proposed methodology based on real-world sensor data from ten tramp trading vessels. The results confirm that over a four-year period, fuel consumption can be reduced by up to 5%, even when accounting for the costs of one or two additional cleaning events.

</details>


### [127] [Wasserstein-enabled characterization of designs and myopic decisions in Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11289)
*Antonio Candelieri,Francesco Archetti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于Wasserstein距离的设计分布特性新表征方法，用于贝叶斯优化中设计信息的模型无关评估，为新一代采集函数提供理论启发


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化的理论收敛证明在现实应用中价值有限，主要受不切实际的假设、固有的短视特性以及初始设计关键作用的影响。需要新的设计表征方法来评估设计的信息价值

Method: 提出基于Wasserstein距离的设计分布特性表征方法，分别测量搜索空间覆盖度和最优观测值周围的集中度。采用经验方法分析设计与下一查询质量的关系

Result: 建立了设计分布特性与下一查询质量之间的经验关系，提供了对设计信息价值评估的新见解

Conclusion: 提出的设计表征方法能够模型无关地评估设计信息价值，为贝叶斯优化新一代采集函数的定义提供了重要启发

Abstract: Impractical assumptions, an inherently myopic nature, and the crucial role of the initial design, all together contribute to making theoretical convergence proofs of little value in real-life Bayesian Optimization applications. In this paper, we propose a novel characterization of the design depending on its distributional properties, separately measured with respect to the coverage of the search space and the concentration around the best observed function value. These measures are based on the Wasserstein distance and enable a model-free evaluation of the information value of the design before deciding the next query. Then, embracing the myopic nature of Bayesian Optimization, we take an empirical approach to analyze the relation between the proposed characterization of the design and the quality of the next query. Ultimately, we provide important and useful insights that might inspire the definition of a new generation of acquisition functions in Bayesian Optimization.

</details>


### [128] [An Efficient Hybrid Heuristic for the Transmission Expansion Planning under Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11490)
*Yure Rocha,Teobaldo Bulhões,Anand Subramanian,Joaquim Dias Garcia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于渐进对冲算法的启发式方法，用于解决考虑可再生能源和需求不确定性的大规模随机输电扩展规划问题，在10000节点系统上验证，相比基准算法平均最优性间隙减少16.23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机输电扩展规划问题面临可再生能源发电容量和需求不确定性的挑战，同时大规模系统的计算复杂度高，需要开发高效的求解方法。

Method: 提出启发式方法，结合渐进对冲算法进行场景分解，并集成破坏修复算子、波束搜索过程和混合整数规划方法来解决子问题。

Result: 在基于加州测试系统参数的多场景大规模系统（最多10000个节点）上评估，相比包含集成MIP和启发式的非平凡基准算法，在2小时时间限制内平均最优性间隙减少16.23%。

Conclusion: 提出的基于渐进对冲算法的框架能有效提高大规模随机输电扩展规划问题的求解质量，显著降低最优性间隙。

Abstract: We address the stochastic transmission expansion planning (STEP) problem considering uncertainties in renewable generation capacity and demand. STEP's objective is to minimize the total investment cost of new transmission lines and generation cost. To tackle the computational challenges of large-scale systems, we propose a heuristic approach that combines the progressive hedging (PH) algorithm for scenario-wise decomposition with an integrated framework for solving the resulting subproblems. The latter combines a destroy-and-repair operator, a beam search procedure, and a mixed-integer programming approach. The proposed framework is evaluated on large-scale systems from the literature, containing up to 10000 nodes, adapted to multiple scenarios based on parameters from the California test system (CATS). Compared with a non-trivial baseline algorithm that includes the integrated MIP and heuristics, the proposed PH-based framework consistently improved solution quality for the six systems considered (including CATS), achieving an average optimality gap reduction of 16.23% within a 2-hour time limit.

</details>


### [129] [Algorithms and Differential Game Representations for Exploring Nonconvex Pareto Fronts in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11515)
*Shanqing Liu,Paula Chen,Youngkyu Lee,Jerome Darbon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于Hamilton-Jacobi方程和微分博弈的新方法，用于探索多目标优化问题的帕累托前沿，能够高效处理高维问题并生成连续解曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多目标优化方法在处理高维问题时面临维度灾难，难以高效探索非凸帕累托前沿。需要开发能够多项式复杂度扩展、生成连续解曲线的新方法。

Method: 将标量化MOO问题嵌入参数化零和博弈的值函数中，其上值解满足一阶HJ方程并具有Hopf-Lax表示公式。通过偏好函数参数化，得到近似解族映射到弱帕累托前沿的稠密子集。

Result: 算法能够缓解维度灾难（决策空间和目标空间维度多项式复杂度扩展），在100维空间中约100秒内暴露非凸帕累托前沿上的连续曲线。数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于HJ方程和微分博弈的新框架为多目标优化提供了高效工具，能够处理高维非凸问题并生成帕累托前沿的连续表示，具有理论和实际应用价值。

Abstract: We develop a new Hamiton-Jacobi (HJ) and differential game approach for exploring the Pareto front of (constrained) multi-objective optimization (MOO) problems. Given a preference function, we embed the scalarized MOO problem into the value function of a parameterized zero-sum game, whose upper value solves a first-order HJ equation that admits a Hopf-Lax representation formula. For each parameter value, this representation yields an inner minimizer that can be interpreted as an approximate solution to a shifted scalarization of the original MOO problem. Under mild assumptions, the resulting family of solutions maps to a dense subset of the weak Pareto front. Finally, we propose a primal-dual algorithm based on this approach for solving the corresponding optimality system. Numerical experiments show that our algorithm mitigates the curse of dimensionality (scaling polynomially with the dimension of the decision and objective spaces) and is able to expose continuous curves along nonconvex Pareto fronts in 100D in just $\sim$100 seconds.

</details>


### [130] [Online Electric Vehicle Charging Control with Battery Thermal Management in Cold Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11561)
*Xiaowei Wang,Yize Chen,Yue Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种面向寒冷环境的电动汽车在线充电控制策略，集成电池热管理，基于队列模型和Lyapunov优化框架，无需预测且对不确定性建模无依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车在寒冷地区推广受限，主要因为低温下电池性能下降，需要有效的电池热管理系统来保障充电效率和电池健康。

Method: 建立电池充电和热动力学的队列模型，构建优化问题协调充电与加热，基于Lyapunov优化理论开发无需预测的在线控制算法。

Result: 理论分析了控制方法的可行性和最优性，基于真实数据的数值仿真验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的在线控制策略能有效协调寒冷环境下电动汽车的充电与热管理，无需预测且对不确定性具有鲁棒性，有助于促进寒冷地区电动汽车的推广。

Abstract: Electric vehicles (EVs) are expanding rapidly, driven by the proposal to comply with global emission reduction targets. However, EV adoption in cold regions is hindered by degraded battery performance at low temperatures, which necessitates effective battery thermal management. Hence, this work proposes a novel online EV charging control strategy, incorporating battery thermal management for cold environments. We first build queue models for both battery charging and thermal dynamics. Then, we formulate an optimization problem, which allows us to coordinate battery charging and heating through maintaining queue stability. To solve the problem, we develop an online control algorithm within the theoretical framework of Lyapunov optimization. Note that our online method is prediction-free and independent of any assumed modeling of uncertainty. We also characterize both the feasibility and optimality of the proposed control approach. Numerical results based on real-world data corroborate our theoretical findings and demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our control method through comparisons.

</details>


### [131] [On a group of invariances in a class of functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11566)
*Shravan Mohan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究一类由多元多项式和整流式单项映射交替组合形成的参数化函数，识别并刻画其非平凡参数不变性，提供不变性结构的构造性描述，实现稀疏函数表示、参数混淆和优化降维


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一类特殊参数化函数族的参数不变性结构，即不同的参数设置可能产生相同的输入-输出映射。这种不变性在深度学习等应用中具有重要意义，但尚未得到系统研究

Method: 研究由多元多项式和整流式单项映射交替组合形成的参数化函数族（层间指数作为固定超参数不进行优化）。识别并刻画这类函数的非平凡参数不变性，提供不变性结构的构造性描述

Result: 成功识别并刻画了该类函数的参数不变性，提供了不变性结构的构造性描述。这些结果可用于实现稀疏函数表示、参数混淆，并为优化过程提供潜在的维度降低

Conclusion: 该研究系统分析了一类特殊参数化函数族的参数不变性结构，为理解深度学习等应用中参数空间的冗余性提供了理论框架，并展示了其在稀疏表示、参数混淆和优化降维方面的应用潜力

Abstract: A class of parametric functions formed by alternating compositions of multivariate polynomials and rectification style monomial maps is studied (the layer-wise exponents are treated as fixed hyperparameters and are not optimized). For this family, nontrivial parametric invariances are identified and characterized, i.e., distinct parameter settings that induce identical input-output maps. A constructive description of the invariance structure is provided, enabling sparse function representations, parameter obfuscation, and potential dimensionality reduction for optimization.

</details>


### [132] [Composite Optimization using Local Models and Global Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11594)
*Welington de Oliveira,Johannes O. Royset*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出统一框架，结合全局近似与局部模型处理非凸非光滑复合优化问题，证明近似问题近稳定点收敛到原问题稳定点，开发实用双层算法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有挑战性的非凸非光滑复合优化问题（包括扩展实值函数情形），现有方法在处理全局近似与局部适应性方面存在局限，需要统一框架来有效结合两者优势

Method: 建立统一框架，将全局近似与局部构建模型相结合；证明近似问题近稳定点在适当条件下收敛到原问题稳定点；开发实用算法，使用从近似问题局部模型导出的可处理凸主程序；采用双层结构，在改进全局近似的同时适应局部模型

Result: 理论证明近似问题近稳定点收敛到原问题稳定点；开发出灵活可实现的算法框架，适用于广泛的复合优化问题类别；为领域内新算法发展奠定基础

Conclusion: 提出的统一框架成功结合全局近似与局部模型，为处理非凸非光滑复合优化问题提供了理论保证和实用算法，具有广泛适用性和灵活性，为后续算法发展奠定基础

Abstract: This work presents a unified framework that combines global approximations with locally built models to handle challenging nonconvex and nonsmooth composite optimization problems, including cases involving extended real-valued functions. We show that near-stationary points of the approximating problems converge to stationary points of the original problem under suitable conditions. Building on this, we develop practical algorithms that use tractable convex master programs derived from local models of the approximating problems. The resulting double-loop structure improves global approximations while adapting local models, providing a flexible and implementable approach for a wide class of composite optimization problems. It also lays the groundwork for new algorithmic developments in this domain.

</details>


### [133] [From Consensus-Based Optimization to Evolution Strategies: Proof of Global Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11677)
*Massimo Fornasier,Hui Huang,Jona Klemenc,Greta Malaspina*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出了三种改进的基于共识的优化(CBO)变体：δ-CBO防止过早收敛，共识冻结方案实现数值稳定，共识跳跃方案作为(1,λ)进化策略，均具有全局收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始CBO方法存在实际和理论限制，包括可能过早收敛到次优状态、数值稳定性问题，以及缺乏对不变测度和收敛速率的理论分析。

Method: 1. δ-CBO：引入非消失扩散项防止过早收敛；2. 共识冻结方案：通过冻结共识点实现任意时间步长的数值稳定；3. 共识跳跃方案：通过时间重标度和渐近分析得到的(1,λ)进化策略算法。

Result: 首次完整表征了这些方案的不变测度，建立了全局收敛理论，包括指数收敛速率，并通过渐近极限连接了不同模型。

Conclusion: 提出的CBO变体解决了原始方法的局限性，提供了理论保证的实用优化算法，特别适用于高维非凸非光滑问题，为基于共识的优化方法建立了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Consensus-based optimization (CBO) is a powerful and versatile zero-order multi-particle method designed to provably solve high-dimensional global optimization problems, including those that are genuinely nonconvex or nonsmooth. The method relies on a balance between stochastic exploration and contraction toward a consensus point, which is defined via the Laplace principle as a proxy for the global minimizer.
  In this paper, we introduce new CBO variants that address practical and theoretical limitations of the original formulation of this novel optimization methodology. First, we propose a model called $δ$-CBO}, which incorporates nonvanishing diffusion to prevent premature collapse to suboptimal states. We also develop a numerically stable implementation, the Consensus Freezing scheme, that remains robust even for arbitrarily large time steps by freezing the consensus point over time intervals. We connect these models through appropriate asymptotic limits. Furthermore, we derive from the Consensus Freezing scheme by suitable time rescaling and asymptotics a further algorithm, the Consensus Hopping scheme, which can be interpreted as a form of $(1,λ)$-Evolution Strategy. For all these schemes, we characterize for the first time the invariant measures and establish global convergence results, including exponential convergence rates.

</details>


### [134] [An objective-function-free algorithm for general smooth constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11770)
*S. Bellavia,S. Gratton,B. Morini,Ph. L. Toint*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种新的光滑约束优化算法，该算法从不计算目标函数值，能处理等式和不等式约束，采用自适应切换策略，在减少约束不可行性的法向步和改进对偶最优性的切向步之间切换，后者受AdaGrad-norm方法启发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种不需要计算目标函数值的约束优化算法，能够同时处理等式和不等式约束，并提高对噪声的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用自适应切换策略：法向步减少约束不可行性，切向步改进对偶最优性（受AdaGrad-norm启发）。算法不计算目标函数值，只使用梯度信息。

Result: 理论分析显示，对于满秩雅可比矩阵的问题，生成梯度的范数以O(1/√(k+1))速率收敛到零。数值实验表明算法对目标函数梯度噪声具有显著不敏感性。

Conclusion: 该算法提供了一种无需计算目标函数值的约束优化方法，具有良好的收敛性和对梯度噪声的鲁棒性，适用于实际应用场景。

Abstract: A new algorithm for smooth constrained optimization is proposed that never computes the value of the problem's objective function and that handles both equality and inequality constraints. The algorithm uses an adaptive switching strategy between a normal step aiming at reducing constraint's infeasibility and a tangential step improving dual optimality, the latter being inspired by the AdaGrad-norm method. Its worst-case iteration complexity is analyzed, showing that the norm of the gradients generated converges to zero like O(1/\sqrt{k+1}) for problems with full-rank Jacobians. Numerical experiments show that the algorithm's performance is remarkably insensitive to noise in the objective function's gradient.

</details>


### [135] [Third-Order Dynamical Systems for Generalized Inverse Mixed Variational Inequality Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11817)
*Nam Van Tran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出并分析了一个三阶动力系统用于求解希尔伯特空间中的广义逆混合变分不等式问题，证明了轨迹的存在唯一性和指数收敛性，并推导出具有双重惯性效应的前向后向算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决希尔伯特空间中广义逆混合变分不等式问题，需要开发高效的数值方法。传统方法可能收敛速度较慢，因此需要设计具有快速收敛特性的动力系统和相应的离散化算法。

Method: 提出了一个三阶动力系统来求解广义逆混合变分不等式问题。在适当的连续性假设下建立了系统轨迹的存在性和唯一性，并在强单调性和Lipschitz连续性条件下证明了轨迹的指数收敛性。进一步推导了该动力系统的显式离散化，得到了具有双重惯性效应的前向后向算法。

Result: 证明了动力系统轨迹的存在唯一性，以及在强单调性和Lipschitz连续性条件下轨迹的指数收敛性。对于离散化算法，建立了生成迭代序列的线性收敛性，确保算法能快速收敛到唯一解。

Conclusion: 提出的三阶动力系统及其离散化算法为求解广义逆混合变分不等式问题提供了有效的数值方法，具有理论保证的收敛性能，特别是指数收敛和线性收敛特性确保了计算效率。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose and analyze a third-order dynamical system for solving a generalized inverse mixed variational inequality problem in a Hilbert space H. We establish the existence and uniqueness of the trajectories generated by the system under suitable continuity assumptions, and prove their exponential convergence to the unique solution under strong monotonicity and Lipschitz continuity conditions. Furthermore, we derive an explicit discretization of the proposed dynamical system, leading to a forward -backward algorithm with double inertial effects. We then establish the linear convergence of the generated iterates to the unique solution.

</details>


### [136] [Multi-period Newsvendor Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11821)
*Valentyn Khokhlov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将报童模型扩展到多周期制造环境，开发用于确定最优生产批量的决策工具，相比传统报童模型和安全库存方法能获得更高利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统报童模型存在两个主要局限：1) 仅适用于单周期场景；2) 主要针对贸易公司而非制造企业。制造企业需要多周期生产批量决策工具，并考虑仓储、损耗、资金成本等间接费用。

Method: 将报童模型扩展为多周期模型，目标函数基于会计准则最大化营业利润，可纳入仓储、损耗、资金成本、生产提前期等间接费用。使用蒙特卡洛模拟验证模型性能。

Result: 1) 相比其他报童模型和安全库存缓冲方法，提出的模型能获得更高利润；2) 关键优势：生产批量适应性更好，相比其他报童模型减少缺货，相比安全库存方法降低库存水平；3) 模型对需求分布假设的误配具有较强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该多周期报童模型为制造企业提供了有效的生产批量决策工具，能提高盈利能力。论文还提供了针对不同SKU选择合适"模型"分布的建议。

Abstract: The newsvendor model is a well-known stochastic model for inventory management; however, it was originally developed for a single-period context and focuses on trading companies. This paper proposes an extension of the newsvendor model into a mutli-period setting, aiming to develop a decision-making tool for manufacturing firms to determine the optimal production batch size. The objective function is to maximize operating profit in accordance with generally accepted accounting principles. The model can also incorporate overhead costs, such as warehousing, shrinkage, cost of capital, and lead time between the production decision and output. Monte Carlo simulations demonstrate that the proposed model results in higher profitability compared to other newsvendor models used in our analysis, as well as the safety stock buffer approach. The key feature explaining its outperformance is better adaptability of the production batch size, that leads to fewer stock-outs relative to other newsvendor models and lower inventory levels compared to the safety stock buffer approach. The robustness analysis shows that the proposed model is quite tolerant of mismatches between the "model" and the "true" demand distributions. Finally, we provide some recommendations on selecting the appropriate "model" distribution for different SKUs.

</details>


### [137] [A High-Performance Parallel Algorithm for Multi-Objective Integer Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11872)
*Kathrin Prinz,Levin Nemesch,Stefan Ruzika*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: PEA是首个充分利用现代硬件多核架构的精确算法，用于解决多目标整数优化问题，能处理比现有算法大得多的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标整数优化问题难以求解，主要因为非支配解的数量通常极其庞大，需要更高效的算法来处理大规模实例。

Method: PEA算法通过利用底层标量化参数集的结构，能够使用大量线程同时避免并行计算的常见陷阱，具有高度可扩展性和易于实现的特点。

Result: PEA能够解决比先前最先进算法大得多的实例，并且在计算研究中展示了其潜力，同时保持与顺序算法相同的标量化问题求解数量。

Conclusion: PEA是首个充分利用多核架构的精确算法，具有坚实的理论基础，在解决大规模多目标整数优化问题方面表现出色。

Abstract: Multi-objective integer optimization problems are hard to solve, mainly because the number of nondominated images is often extremely large. We present the first exact algorithm, called PEA, that fully utilizes the multicore architecture of modern hardware. By exploiting the structure of the parameter set of the underlying scalarization, PEA can use a high number of threads while avoiding the usual pitfalls of parallel computing. It is highly scalable and easy to implement. As a result, PEA can solve much larger instances than previous state-of-the-art algorithms. Besides, PEA has a sound theoretical foundation. Unlike other existing parallel algorithms, it always solves the same number of scalarization problems as comparable sequential algorithms. We demonstrate the potential of PEA in a computational study.

</details>


### [138] [Mixed-Integer Programming for Change-point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11947)
*Apoorva Narula,Santanu S. Dey,Yao Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于混合整数规划的多变点检测新方法，通过分段线性拟合将问题转化为全局最优求解，并开发了强化MIP公式，其线性规划松弛在分段分配变量上具有积分投影特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线多变点检测方法在计算效率和理论保证方面存在局限，需要开发具有更强理论保证和更好计算性能的全局最优方法。

Method: 将多变点检测问题建模为全局最优分段线性拟合问题，提出一族强化的混合整数规划公式，其线性规划松弛在分段分配变量上具有积分投影特性，从而获得比现有方法更紧的松弛。进一步将框架扩展到多维共享变点模型和稀疏变点检测场景。

Result: 在真实世界基准数据集上的大量计算实验表明，在ℓ₁和ℓ₂损失函数下，所提公式相比现有最优方法显著减少了求解时间。

Conclusion: 提出的强化MIP公式为离线多变点检测提供了具有更强理论保证和更好计算性能的新方法，特别适用于多维共享变点和稀疏变点检测等复杂场景。

Abstract: We present a new mixed-integer programming (MIP) approach for offline multiple change-point detection by casting the problem as a globally optimal piecewise linear (PWL) fitting problem. Our main contribution is a family of strengthened MIP formulations whose linear programming (LP) relaxations admit integral projections onto the segment assignment variables, which encode the segment membership of each data point. This property yields provably tighter relaxations than existing formulations for offline multiple change-point detection. We further extend the framework to two settings of active research interest: (i) multidimensional PWL models with shared change-points, and (ii) sparse change-point detection, where only a subset of dimensions undergo structural change. Extensive computational experiments on benchmark real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed formulations achieve reductions in solution times under both $\ell_1$ and $\ell_2$ loss functions in comparison to the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [139] [Local convergence of mean-field Langevin dynamics: from gradient flows to linearly monotone games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11999)
*Guillaume Wang,Lénaïc Chizat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究扩散平均场系统的局部收敛性，包括Wasserstein梯度流、min-max动力学和多智能体博弈，在Poincaré不等式和单调性条件下建立χ²散度的指数局部收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非梯度系统（如博弈）的收敛性，解决COLT 2024提出的开放性问题，扩展传统梯度流收敛理论的应用范围

Method: 设计混合χ²散度和加权负Sobolev范数的Lyapunov泛函，在Poincaré不等式和单调性条件下分析局部收敛性

Result: 获得梯度流情况下由Poincaré常数控制的尖锐收敛速率，并首次为线性单调多玩家博弈提供χ²散度的局部指数收敛速率

Conclusion: 该方法统一处理梯度流和非梯度系统，为扩散平均场系统的局部收敛分析提供了新框架，部分回答了COLT 2024的开放性问题

Abstract: We study the local convergence of diffusive mean-field systems, including Wasserstein gradient flows, min-max dynamics, and multi-species games. We establish exponential local convergence in $χ^2$-divergence with sharp rates, under two main assumptions: (i) the stationary measures satisfy a Poincaré inequality, and (ii) the velocity field satisfies a monotonicity condition, which reduces to linear convexity of the objective in the gradient flow case. We do not assume any form of displacement convexity or displacement monotonicity.
  In the gradient flow case, global exponential convergence is already known under our linear convexity assumption, with an asymptotic rate governed by the log-Sobolev constant of the stationary measure. Our contribution in this setting is to identify the sharp rate near equilibrium governed instead by the Poincaré constant. This rate coincides with the one suggested by Otto calculus (i.e. by a tight positivity estimate of the Wasserstein Hessian), and refines some results of Tamura (1984), extending them beyond quadratic objectives.
  More importantly, our proof technique extends to certain non-gradient systems, such as linearly monotone two-player and multi-player games. In this case, we obtain explicit local exponential convergence rates in $χ^2$-divergence, thereby partially answering the open question raised by the authors at COLT 2024. While that question concerns global convergence (which remains open), even local convergence results were previously unavailable.
  At the heart of our analysis is the design of a Lyapunov functional that mixes the $χ^2$-divergence with weighted negative Sobolev norms of the density relative to equilibrium.

</details>


### [140] [The colored knapsack problem: structural properties and exact algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12214)
*Fabio Ciccarelli,Alexander Helber,Erik Mühmer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了经典背包问题的新泛化——彩色背包问题，其中物品按颜色分类，要求打包物品的排列中相邻物品颜色不同。证明了问题的弱NP难性，提出了两种精确动态规划算法，并建立了理论分析和实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典背包问题在实际应用中存在限制，需要处理具有颜色约束的场景，如物流、调度等领域中相邻物品不能同色的需求。彩色背包问题填补了这一研究空白，为带有顺序约束的背包问题提供了理论框架。

Method: 1. 证明CKP是弱NP难问题；2. 提出两种精确动态规划算法，时间复杂度分别为O(bn⁴)和O(b²n³)；3. 推导多种支配和剪枝规则提升算法性能；4. 分析线性规划松弛，证明最优解最多有两个分数项；5. 建立基于彩色装箱问题的CKP实例基准。

Result: 1. CKP被证明是弱NP难问题；2. 线性规划松弛可在O(n)时间内求解，与经典KP复杂度相同；3. 提出的动态规划算法在大多数实例上显著优于最先进的MIP求解器；4. 建立了全面的CKP实例基准。

Conclusion: 彩色背包问题是经典背包问题的有效泛化，具有实际应用价值。提出的动态规划算法在理论和实践上都表现出色，为解决带有颜色约束的背包问题提供了有效工具。线性规划松弛的高效求解为近似算法设计奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce and study a novel generalization of the classical Knapsack Problem (KP), called the Colored Knapsack Problem (CKP). In this problem, the items are partitioned into classes of colors and the packed items need to be ordered such that no consecutive items are of the same color. We establish that the problem is weakly NP-hard and propose two exact dynamic programming algorithms with time complexities of $\mathcal{O}(bn^4)$ and $\mathcal{O}(b^2n^3)$, respectively. To enhance practical performance, we derive various dominance and fathoming rules for both approaches. From a theoretical perspective, we analyze the linear programming relaxation of the natural CKP formulation, proving that an optimal solution exists with at most two fractional items. We also show that the relaxation can be solved in $\mathcal{O}(n)$ time, matching the complexity of the classical KP. Finally, we establish a comprehensive benchmark of CKP instances, derived from the Colored Bin Packing Problem. Extensive computational experiments demonstrate that the proposed dynamic programming algorithms significantly outperform state-of-the-art MIP solvers on most of these instances.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Automated Optimization Modeling via a Localizable Error-Driven Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11164)
*Weiting Liu,Han Wu,Yufei Kuang,Xiongwei Han,Tao Zhong,Jianfeng Feng,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIND提出了一种基于错误驱动学习的自动化优化建模框架，通过定位错误传播模式和动态监督微调策略优化，解决了现有方法中错误特定问题稀疏和困难问题奖励稀疏的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动化优化建模方法在领域特定后训练中面临两个根本限制：错误特定问题的稀疏性（L1）和困难问题相关奖励的稀疏性（L2），导致后训练效果受限。

Method: 提出MIND框架，基于优化建模错误传播的局部化模式观察，构建高密度训练语料库，并设计动态监督微调策略优化（DFPO），通过局部化精炼处理困难问题。

Result: 在六个基准测试上的实验表明，MIND在所有最先进的自动化优化建模方法中表现一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MIND通过错误驱动学习框架有效解决了自动化优化建模中的数据稀缺和利用不足问题，为复杂决策支持提供了更有效的LLM后训练方法。

Abstract: Automated optimization modeling via Large Language Models (LLMs) has emerged as a promising approach to assist complex human decision-making. While post-training has become a pivotal technique to enhance LLMs' capabilities in this domain, its effectiveness is severely constrained by the scarcity and underutilization of high-quality training data. However, through a detailed profiling of error patterns across various problem-response pairs drawn from post-training, we identify two fundamental limitations of existing automated optimization modeling approaches: (L1) the sparsity of error-specific problems and (L2) the sparse rewards associated with difficult problems. We demonstrate that these limitations can result in suboptimal performance in domain-specific post-training for LLMs. To tackle the above two limitations, we propose a novel error-driven learning framework -- namely, auto\textbf{m}ated opt\textbf{i}mization modeli\textbf{n}g via a localizable error-\textbf{d}riven perspective (MIND) -- that customizes the whole model training framework from data synthesis to post-training. MIND is based on our key observation of the unique localizable patterns in error propagation of optimization modelings, that is, modeling errors may remain localized to specific semantic segments and do not propagate throughout the entire solution. Thus, in contrast to holistic reasoning tasks such as mathematical proofs, MIND leverages the construction of a focused, high-density training corpus and proposes \textbf{D}ynamic Supervised \textbf{F}ine-Tuning \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (DFPO) to tackle difficult problems through localized refinement. Experiments on six benchmarks demonstrate that MIND consistently outperforms all the state-of-the-art automated optimization modeling approaches.

</details>


### [142] [KBVQ-MoE: KLT-guided SVD with Bias-Corrected Vector Quantization for MoE Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11184)
*Zukang Xu,Zhixiong Zhao,Xing Hu,Zhixuan Chen,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KBVQ-MoE：一种针对MoE模型的新型向量量化框架，通过消除专家间冗余表示和校正输出偏置，显著提升极低位量化性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽然通过稀疏专家激活实现了计算效率，但其巨大的参数量和内存需求在资源受限环境中部署面临挑战。传统向量量化直接应用于MoE会导致性能显著下降，主要原因是专家间冗余表示导致码本容量利用低效，以及专家聚合放大了累积输出偏置。

Method: 提出KBVQ-MoE框架，包含两项关键技术：1）输入驱动的冗余消除：使用KLT引导的奇异值分解提取主导权重分量并在专家间共享；2）偏置校正的输出稳定：仅对专家特定（非冗余）表示应用向量量化，并通过通道级仿射补偿校正量化输出。

Result: 在各种MoE LLM上的实验表明，KBVQ-MoE比现有量化方法显著更好地保持精度。例如，Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B的3位量化达到平均精度67.99，接近FP16基线的68.07。

Conclusion: KBVQ-MoE通过有效解决MoE模型量化中的冗余表示和输出偏置问题，实现了高效的极低位压缩，为边缘设备等资源受限平台上的MoE模型部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Mixture of Experts (MoE) models have achieved great success by significantly improving performance while maintaining computational efficiency through sparse expert activation. However, their enormous parameter sizes and memory demands pose major challenges for deployment in resource-constrained environments. Vector Quantization (VQ) offers a promising approach for ultra-low-bit compression in Large Language Models (LLMs) by leveraging a codebook, where weight vectors are mapped to the most similar discrete codewords. Yet, directly applying VQ to MoEs often leads to substantial performance degradation due to two critical obstacles: (1) redundant representations among experts cause VQ to repeatedly quantize similar representations for each expert, resulting in inefficient use of limited codebook capacity; and (2) cumulative output bias is amplified by expert aggregation in MoE layers, leading to distributional shifts in the quantized outputs. To address these issues, we propose KBVQ-MoE, a novel VQ framework to enhance extremely low-bit quantization for MoE-based LLMs. KBVQ-MoE integrates two techniques: (1) input-driven redundancy elimination, where a Karhunen-Loeve Transform (KLT) guided singular value decomposition (SVD) extracts dominant weight components and shares them across experts; and (2) bias-corrected output stabilization, where vector quantization is applied only to expert-specific (non-redundant) representations and the quantized outputs are corrected via channel-wise affine compensation. Experiments on various MoE LLMs demonstrate that KBVQ-MoE preserves accuracy substantially better than existing quantization methods. For example, 3-bit quantization of Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B achieves an average accuracy of 67.99, nearly identical to the FP16 baseline of 68.07, underscoring KBVQ-MoE's potential for efficient deployment on edge devices and other resource-constrained platforms.

</details>


### [143] [Spectra: Rethinking Optimizers for LLMs Under Spectral Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11185)
*Zhendong Huang,Hengjie Cao,Fang Dong,Ruijun Huang,Mengyi Chen,Yifeng Yang,Xin Zhang,Anrui Chen,Mingzhi Dong,Yujiang Wang,Jinlong Hou,Qin Lv,Robert P. Dick,Yuan Cheng,Fan Yang,Tun Lu,Li Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Spectra优化器通过抑制LLM梯度中的主导低秩尖峰子空间，加速训练并减少内存占用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM训练中的梯度信号高度各向异性：循环语言结构将能量集中在少数主导谱方向（尖峰），而上下文特定信息位于长尾中。尖峰主导优化器统计量，抑制了尾部的学习

Method: 提出Spectra优化器：通过缓存的、热启动的幂迭代跟踪尖峰子空间，应用低秩谱整形，抑制主导低秩尖峰子空间而不放大噪声敏感的谱尾部

Result: 在LLaMA3 8B训练50B tokens上：比AdamW快30%达到相同目标损失，端到端开销减少0.7%，优化器状态内存减少49.25%，下游平均准确率提高1.62%；相比Muon优化器处理时间快5.1倍，最终损失更低，平均准确率提高0.66%

Conclusion: 梯度信号的尖峰-尾部分离现象在LLM训练中持续存在，Spectra通过针对性的谱整形有效解决了这一问题，显著提升了训练效率和模型性能

Abstract: Gradient signals in LLM training are highly anisotropic: recurrent linguistic structure concentrates energy into a small set of dominant spectral directions, while context specific information resides in a long tail. We show that this spike tail separation persists throughout training, with the spike occupying only about 1.5% of directions yet dominating optimizer statistics. This dominance suppresses tail learning by contracting tail updates through second moment normalization and tightening the globally stable learning rate bound. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Spectra, a spike aware optimizer that suppresses the dominant low rank spike subspace without amplifying the noise sensitive spectral tail. Spectra tracks the spike subspace via cached, warm started power iteration and applies low rank spectral shaping with negligible overhead and substantially reduced optimizer state memory. On LLaMA3 8B trained on 50B tokens, Spectra reaches the same target loss 30% faster than AdamW, reduces per step end to end overhead by 0.7%, cuts optimizer state memory by 49.25%, and improves average downstream accuracy by 1.62%. Compared to Muon, Spectra is 5.1x faster in optimizer processing time, achieves a lower final loss, and improves average accuracy by 0.66%.

</details>


### [144] [TDPNavigator-Placer: Thermal- and Wirelength-Aware Chiplet Placement in 2.5D Systems Through Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11187)
*Yubo Hou,Furen Zhuang,Partha Pratim Kundu,Sezin Ata Kircali,Jie Wang,Mihai Dragos Rotaru,Dutta Rahul,Ashish James*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TDPNavigator-Placer：基于热设计功率的多智能体强化学习框架，用于优化2.5D集成电路中小芯片布局，解决线长与热管理的冲突目标


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有布局方法通常专注于最小化线长或将多目标优化转化为加权和的单目标，限制了处理竞争设计需求的能力。线长减少和热管理本质上是冲突的目标，使得先前方法在实际部署中不足

Method: 提出TDPNavigator-Placer，一种新颖的多智能体强化学习框架，基于小芯片的热设计功率动态优化布局。该方法明确将这些本质冲突的目标分配给专门的智能体，每个智能体在统一的布局范式下运行不同的奖励机制和环境约束

Result: 实验结果表明，TDPNavigator-Placer相比最先进方法提供了显著改进的帕累托前沿，实现了线长和热性能之间更平衡的权衡

Conclusion: TDPNavigator-Placer通过多智能体强化学习框架有效解决了2.5D集成电路布局中线长与热管理的冲突优化问题，为实际部署提供了更优的解决方案

Abstract: The rapid growth of electronics has accelerated the adoption of 2.5D integrated circuits, where effective automated chiplet placement is essential as systems scale to larger and more heterogeneous chiplet assemblies. Existing placement methods typically focus on minimizing wirelength or transforming multi-objective optimization into a single objective through weighted sum, which limits their ability to handle competing design requirements. Wirelength reduction and thermal management are inherently conflicting objectives, making prior approaches inadequate for practical deployment. To address this challenge, we propose TDPNavigator-Placer, a novel multi-agent reinforcement learning framework that dynamically optimizes placement based on chiplet's thermal design power (TDP). This approach explicitly assigns these inherently conflicting objectives to specialized agents, each operating under distinct reward mechanisms and environmental constraints within a unified placement paradigm. Experimental results demonstrate that TDPNavigator-Placer delivers a significantly improved Pareto front over state-of-the-art methods, enabling more balanced trade-offs between wirelength and thermal performance.

</details>


### [145] [MELINOE: Fine-Tuning Enables Memory-Efficient Inference for Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11192)
*Arian Raje,Anupam Nayak,Gauri Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MELINOE通过微调MoE模型使其更倾向于激活更少的专家，从而减少专家切换和CPU-GPU传输开销，显著提升推理效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽然能减少每个token激活的参数数量，但整体参数规模庞大，在资源受限环境中需要将所有参数加载到GPU内存中，造成内存瓶颈。现有方法通过将部分专家卸载到CPU内存并在激活时传输，但面临显著的I/O延迟问题。

Method: 提出MELINOE方法，通过微调MoE模型使其在序列处理中更强烈地倾向于激活更少数量的专家。将这些偏好的专家缓存在GPU内存中，减少专家切换和CPU-GPU传输开销。

Result: MELINOE相比高效基线提升了1.2-3倍吞吐量，相比传输密集型基线提升高达14.7倍，同时在下游任务中保持甚至改善了模型性能。

Conclusion: MELINOE是一种可靠的方法，能够显著改善MoE模型的推理效率，通过在GPU内存中缓存偏好的专家来减少专家切换和传输开销，同时保持模型性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model architectures can significantly reduce the number of activated parameters per token, enabling computationally efficient training and inference. However, their large overall parameter counts and model sizes have precluded their widespread usage in resource-constrained settings as all of the parameters must still be loaded into GPU memory. Prior works aim to address this memory bottleneck by offloading certain experts into CPU memory and porting them to GPU memory only when they are activated. In practice, these methods suffer from the significant I/O latency incurred by expert transfer. We present MELINOE, a method that fine-tunes an MoE model to more strongly prefer activating a smaller number of experts per sequence. Caching these preferred experts in GPU memory reduces expert churn and CPU-GPU transfer overhead. MELINOE increases throughput by $1.2-3\times$ over efficient baselines and up to $14.7\times$ over transfer-heavy baselines while retaining or even improving the performance of the model on a downstream task, making it a reliable method for improving MoE inference efficiency.

</details>


### [146] [Predicting the post-wildfire mudflow onset using machine learning models on multi-parameter experimental data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11194)
*Mahta Movasat,Ingrid Tomac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究应用多种机器学习算法预测和分类实验室模拟的野火后泥石流，发现MLR能有效预测总流量但侵蚀预测较差，LR和SVC在失效分类中表现良好，敏感性分析显示细砂在低强度长历时降雨下最易受侵蚀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火后泥石流日益危险，土壤疏水性增强侵蚀，导致与自然泥流不同的高强度、长历时破坏性泥石流。需要理解泥石流启动的时机和条件，这些受降雨强度、坡度、入渗值和粒径等关键参数耦合影响。

Method: 应用多种机器学习算法：多元线性回归(MLR)、逻辑回归(LR)、支持向量分类器(SVC)、K-means聚类和主成分分析(PCA)，预测和分类实验室实验结果。实验使用降雨装置在不同土壤的倾斜水槽中模拟现场条件。

Result: MLR能有效预测总流量，但对侵蚀的预测准确性较差，特别是粗砂。LR和SVC在失效结果分类中达到良好准确率，得到聚类和降维支持。敏感性分析显示细砂极易受侵蚀，特别是在低强度长历时降雨条件下。结果还表明高强度降雨的前10分钟对流量和失效最为关键。

Conclusion: 机器学习在野火后泥石流灾害评估和应急响应规划中具有潜力，能够有效预测和分类泥石流发生条件，为灾害管理提供科学依据。

Abstract: Post-wildfire mudflows are increasingly hazardous due to the prevalence of wildfires, including those on the wildland-urban interface. Upon burning, soil on the surface or immediately beneath becomes hydrophobic, a phenomenon that occurs predominantly on sand-based hillslopes. Rainwater and eroded soil blanket the downslope, leading to catastrophic debris flows. Soil hydrophobicity enhances erosion, resulting in post-wildfire debris flows that differ from natural mudflows in intensity, duration, and destructiveness. Thus, it is crucial to understand the timing and conditions of debris-flow onset, driven by the coupled effects of critical parameters: varying rain intensities (RI), slope gradients, water-entry values, and grain sizes (D50). Machine Learning (ML) techniques have become increasingly valuable in geotechnical engineering due to their ability to model complex systems without predefined assumptions. This study applies multiple ML algorithms: multiple linear regression (MLR), logistic regression (LR), support vector classifier (SVC), K-means clustering, and principal component analysis (PCA) to predict and classify outcomes from laboratory experiments that model field conditions using a rain device on various soils in sloped flumes. While MLR effectively predicted total discharge, erosion predictions were less accurate, especially for coarse sand. LR and SVC achieved good accuracy in classifying failure outcomes, supported by clustering and dimensionality reduction. Sensitivity analysis revealed that fine sand is highly susceptible to erosion, particularly under low-intensity, long-duration rainfall. Results also show that the first 10 minutes of high-intensity rain are most critical for discharge and failure. These findings highlight the potential of ML for post-wildfire hazard assessment and emergency response planning.

</details>


### [147] [AM-FM: A Foundation Model for Ambient Intelligence Through WiFi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11200)
*Guozhen Zhu,Yuqian Hu,Sakila Jayaweera,Weihang Gao,Wei-Hsiang Wang,Jiaxuan Zhang,Beibei Wang,Chenshu Wu,K. J. Ray Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AM-FM是首个基于WiFi的泛在智能基础模型，通过对比学习、掩码重建和物理信息目标在920万未标记CSI样本上预训练，在9个下游任务中展现强大跨任务性能和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WiFi基础设施为物理空间中的存在、活动和生理监测提供了无处不在、始终在线且保护隐私的底层支持，但现有无线感知模型通常依赖任务特定模型，需要大量标记数据，限制了实际部署。

Method: AM-FM是首个基于WiFi的泛在智能基础模型，在439天内从20种商用设备类型收集的920万未标记CSI样本上进行预训练，采用对比学习、掩码重建和针对无线信号定制的物理信息目标来学习通用表示。

Result: 在涵盖9个下游任务的公共基准测试中，AM-FM展现出强大的跨任务性能和改进的数据效率，证明基础模型能够利用现有无线基础设施实现可扩展的泛在智能。

Conclusion: AM-FM作为首个WiFi泛在智能基础模型，通过大规模未标记数据预训练学习通用表示，显著提升了跨任务性能和数据效率，为利用现有无线基础设施实现可扩展泛在智能提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Ambient intelligence, continuously understanding human presence, activity, and physiology in physical spaces, is fundamental to smart environments, health monitoring, and human-computer interaction. WiFi infrastructure provides a ubiquitous, always-on, privacy-preserving substrate for this capability across billions of IoT devices. Yet this potential remains largely untapped, as wireless sensing has typically relied on task-specific models that require substantial labeled data and limit practical deployment. We present AM-FM, the first foundation model for ambient intelligence and sensing through WiFi. AM-FM is pre-trained on 9.2 million unlabeled Channel State Information (CSI) samples collected over 439 days from 20 commercial device types deployed worldwide, learning general-purpose representations via contrastive learning, masked reconstruction, and physics-informed objectives tailored to wireless signals. Evaluated on public benchmarks spanning nine downstream tasks, AM-FM shows strong cross-task performance with improved data efficiency, demonstrating that foundation models can enable scalable ambient intelligence using existing wireless infrastructure.

</details>


### [148] [Zero-Sacrifice Persistent-Robustness Adversarial Defense for Pre-Trained Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11204)
*Zhuxin Lei,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZePAD是一种针对自监督学习预训练编码器下游无关对抗样本的防御方法，通过双分支结构实现单次调优即可保护多种下游任务，同时保持良性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习预训练编码器存在下游无关对抗样本的脆弱性，现有防御方法依赖任务特定的对抗微调，导致泛化性受限、灾难性遗忘和良性性能下降。

Method: 提出ZePAD双分支结构：1) MPAE-Branch使用两个对抗微调编码器增强对抗抵抗；2) BMP-Branch在本地数据上训练确保对抗鲁棒性不损害良性性能。该方法无需训练对抗样本识别任务即可直接检测对抗样本。

Result: 在11种SSL方法和6个数据集上的实验验证了有效性，某些情况下良性性能提升29.20%，对抗鲁棒性增益73.86%，实现了零牺牲特性。

Conclusion: ZePAD通过双分支结构和特征多样性增强，实现了单次对抗微调即可防御多种下游任务的对抗样本，同时保持良性性能，达到了持久鲁棒性。

Abstract: The widespread use of publicly available pre-trained encoders from self-supervised learning (SSL) has exposed a critical vulnerability: their susceptibility to downstream-agnostic adversarial examples (DAEs), which are crafted without knowledge of the downstream tasks but capable of misleading downstream models. While several defense methods have been explored recently, they rely primarily on task-specific adversarial fine-tuning, which inevitably limits generalizability and causes catastrophic forgetting and deteriorates benign performance. Different with previous works, we propose a more rigorous defense goal that requires only a single tuning for diverse downstream tasks to defend against DAEs and preserve benign performance. To achieve this defense goal, we introduce Zero-Sacrifice Persistent-Robustness Adversarial Defense (ZePAD), which is inspired by the inherent sensitivity of neural networks to data characteristics. Specifically, ZePAD is a dual-branch structure, which consists of a Multi-Pattern Adversarial Enhancement Branch (MPAE-Branch) that uses two adversarially fine-tuned encoders to strengthen adversarial resistance. The Benign Memory Preservation Branch (BMP-Branch) is trained on local data to ensure adversarial robustness does not compromise benign performance. Surprisingly, we find that ZePAD can directly detect DAEs by evaluating branch confidence, without introducing any adversarial exsample identification task during training. Notably, by enriching feature diversity, our method enables a single adversarial fine-tuning to defend against DAEs across downstream tasks, thereby achieving persistent robustness. Extensive experiments on 11 SSL methods and 6 datasets validate its effectiveness. In certain cases, it achieves a 29.20% improvement in benign performance and a 73.86% gain in adversarial robustness, highlighting its zero-sacrifice property.

</details>


### [149] [Adaptive Physics Transformer with Fused Global-Local Attention for Subsurface Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11208)
*Xin Ju,Nok Hei,Fung,Yuyan Zhang,Carl Jacquemyn,Matthew Jackson,Randolph Settgast,Sally M. Benson,Gege Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应物理Transformer（APT），一种几何、网格和物理无关的神经算子，用于高效模拟地下系统，解决传统全物理数值模拟计算成本高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下系统是现代社会的基石，提供能源资源和CO₂封存场所，但传统全物理数值模拟因地质异质性、高分辨率需求和不同时间尺度物理过程的紧密耦合而计算成本极高。

Method: APT融合图编码器提取高分辨率局部异质特征与全局注意力机制解决长程物理影响，是几何、网格和物理无关的神经算子，可直接从自适应网格细化模拟中学习。

Result: APT在规则和不规则网格的地下任务中优于最先进架构，具有鲁棒的超分辨率能力，是首个直接从自适应网格细化模拟学习的架构，展示跨数据集学习能力。

Conclusion: APT为解决地下系统模拟的计算挑战提供了强大工具，可作为大规模地下基础模型开发的鲁棒可扩展骨干架构。

Abstract: The Earth's subsurface is a cornerstone of modern society, providing essential energy resources like hydrocarbons, geothermal, and minerals while serving as the primary reservoir for $CO_2$ sequestration. However, full physics numerical simulations of these systems are notoriously computationally expensive due to geological heterogeneity, high resolution requirements, and the tight coupling of physical processes with distinct propagation time scales. Here we propose the \textbf{Adaptive Physics Transformer} (APT), a geometry-, mesh-, and physics-agnostic neural operator that explicitly addresses these challenges. APT fuses a graph-based encoder to extract high-resolution local heterogeneous features with a global attention mechanism to resolve long-range physical impacts. Our results demonstrate that APT outperforms state-of-the-art architectures in subsurface tasks across both regular and irregular grids with robust super-resolution capabilities. Notably, APT is the first architecture that directly learns from adaptive mesh refinement simulations. We also demonstrate APT's capability for cross-dataset learning, positioning it as a robust and scalable backbone for large-scale subsurface foundation model development.

</details>


### [150] [Towards Compressive and Scalable Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11212)
*Yunchong Song,Jushi Kai,Liming Lu,Kaixi Qiu,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Elastic Memory是一种基于HiPPO框架的新型记忆架构，通过在线压缩历史序列为固定大小的记忆状态，并使用多项式采样机制进行检索，有效解决了Transformer在长上下文中的二次瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在处理长上下文时面临注意力机制的二次复杂度瓶颈。现有基于循环记忆的方法在理论原则和实际可扩展性之间存在根本性权衡，需要一种既能保持理论严谨性又能实际扩展的解决方案。

Method: 提出Elastic Memory架构：1) 基于HiPPO框架将历史序列视为连续信号的采样，应用最优在线压缩编码为固定大小的记忆状态；2) 提出灵活的多项式采样机制从压缩状态重构历史摘要；3) 采用解耦设计，允许在测试时注入归纳偏置以提升性能。

Result: 在三个领域的32k+长上下文数据集上一致优于基线：1) 同等参数量下，比Memorizing Transformer内存效率高16倍；2) 在所有内存大小下均优于Melodi，即使Melodi参数量多30%；3) 扩展模型大小时保持领先所有基线，在4倍大小时显著快于Melodi。

Conclusion: Elastic Memory通过结合HiPPO框架的理论基础和灵活的多项式采样机制，有效解决了长上下文Transformer的记忆扩展问题，在理论严谨性和实际性能之间取得了良好平衡，为长序列建模提供了高效可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Transformers face a quadratic bottleneck in attention when scaling to long contexts. Recent approaches introduce recurrent memory to extend context beyond the current window, yet these often face a fundamental trade-off between theoretical principles and practical scalability. To address this, we introduce Elastic Memory, a novel memory architecture grounded in the HiPPO framework for online function approximation. Elastic Memory treats historical sequence as samples from continuous signals, applying optimal online compression to encode them into a fixed-size memory state. For retrieval, we propose a flexible \textit{polynomial sampling} mechanism that reconstructs a history summary from this compressed state. Elastic Memory consistently outperformed baselines on long-context (32k+) datasets across three domains. With equal parameters, it beat Memorizing Transformer by 16x memory and outperformed Melodi at all memory sizes, even when Melodi had 30% more parameters. When scaling model size, Elastic Memory stayed ahead of all baselines and was significantly faster than Melodi at 4x size. Furthermore, its decoupled design allows for injecting inductive biases at test-time to boost performance.

</details>


### [151] [Charting Empirical Laws for LLM Fine-Tuning in Scientific Multi-Discipline Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11215)
*Lintao Wang,Zhuqiang Lu,Yilin Zhu,Kun Hu,Zhenfei Yin,Shixiang Tang,Zhiyong Wang,Wanli Ouyang,Xinzhu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了多学科LLM微调，通过构建五学科语料库分析不同微调方法的学习模式，提炼出四条实证法则，为多学科微调提供实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs在单一科学领域通过微调取得了良好性能，但在多学科背景下的学习动态仍不清楚，而跨领域知识协同有望提升泛化能力和更广泛适用性。

Method: 构建五学科语料库，系统分析全参数微调、LoRA、LoRA-MoE和LoRA组合的学习模式，研究多学科微调中的学习动态。

Result: 发现多学科学习比单学科训练更具变异性，并提炼出四条一致实证法则：平衡后多样性、合并后对齐、优化后扩展、共享后专业化，这些法则形成了多学科微调的实际配方。

Conclusion: 这些法则为原则性多学科微调提供了实用配方，并为开发可泛化的科学LLMs提供了可操作的指导，揭示了多学科学习的关键机制。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance through fine-tuning within individual scientific domains, their learning dynamics in multi-disciplinary contexts remains poorly understood, despite the promise of improved generalization and broader applicability through cross-domain knowledge synergy. In this work, we present the first systematic study of multi-disciplinary LLM fine-tuning, constructing a five-discipline corpus and analyzing learning patterns of full fine-tuning, LoRA, LoRA-MoE, and LoRA compositions. Particularly, our study shows that multi-disciplinary learning is substantially more variable than single-discipline training and distills four consistent empirical laws: (1) Balance-then-Diversity: low-resource disciplines degrade performance unless mitigated via diversity-aware upsampling; (2) Merge-then-Align: restoring instruction-following ability is critical for cross-discipline synergy; (3) Optimize-then-Scale: parameter scaling offers limited gains without prior design optimization; and (4) Share-then-Specialize: asymmetric LoRA-MoE yields robust gains with minimal trainable parameters via shared low-rank projection. Together, these laws form a practical recipe for principled multi-discipline fine-tuning and provide actionable guidance for developing generalizable scientific LLMs.

</details>


### [152] [Protein Language Model Embeddings Improve Generalization of Implicit Transfer Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11216)
*Panagiotis Antoniadis,Beatrice Pavesi,Simon Olsson,Ole Winther*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PLaTITO方法通过整合蛋白质语言模型嵌入等辅助信息，提升了可迁移隐式转移算子在分子动力学中的数据效率和泛化能力，在非分布蛋白质系统上实现了最先进的平衡采样性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分子动力学计算成本高，而现有生成式分子动力学方法在跨分子系统迁移性方面有限。本研究旨在通过整合辅助信息源来提升可迁移隐式转移算子的数据效率和泛化能力。

Method: 提出PLaTITO方法，将蛋白质语言模型嵌入等辅助信息整合到可迁移隐式转移算子中，同时研究了结构嵌入、温度和大语言模型衍生嵌入等额外条件信号对模型性能的影响。

Result: 粗粒度TITO模型比Boltzmann Emulators数据效率显著更高；整合pLM嵌入进一步改善了非分布泛化能力；PLaTITO在非分布蛋白质系统（包括快速折叠蛋白）的平衡采样基准测试中实现了最先进性能。

Conclusion: 整合蛋白质语言模型嵌入等辅助信息源能有效提升可迁移隐式转移算子在分子动力学中的数据效率和泛化能力，为高效分子采样提供了有前景的途径。

Abstract: Molecular dynamics (MD) is a central computational tool in physics, chemistry, and biology, enabling quantitative prediction of experimental observables as expectations over high-dimensional molecular distributions such as Boltzmann distributions and transition densities. However, conventional MD is fundamentally limited by the high computational cost required to generate independent samples. Generative molecular dynamics (GenMD) has recently emerged as an alternative, learning surrogates of molecular distributions either from data or through interaction with energy models. While these methods enable efficient sampling, their transferability across molecular systems is often limited. In this work, we show that incorporating auxiliary sources of information can improve the data efficiency and generalization of transferable implicit transfer operators (TITO) for molecular dynamics. We find that coarse-grained TITO models are substantially more data-efficient than Boltzmann Emulators, and that incorporating protein language model (pLM) embeddings further improves out-of-distribution generalization.
